Evaluation:
TheScandinavian Defense (1. e4 d5) is a common opening. White's most common response is to take the pawn (exd5).

If exd5:
Black usually plays ...Nf6 or ... Qxd5.
- If ...Nf6, White can play d4 or Nc3.
- If ...Qxd5, White plays Nc3, gaining a tempo on the Queen.

If e5:
This transitions into something like an Advance French or Caro-Kann structure, but Black hasn't committed to e6 or c6 yet.

If Nc3:
Black can play ...dxe4, and White plays Nxe4.

Standard theory suggests exd5 is the most principled move to maintain center control or force Black to spend time recapturing.
